  const [dishName, setDishName] = useState('');
  const [dishFoodId, setDishFoodId] = useState('');
  const [dishGrams, setDishGrams] = useState('100');
  const [dishIngredients, setDishIngredients] = useState<Array<{ foodId: string; grams: number }>>([]);
  const [zone, setZone] = useState({ title: '', areas: '', serviceHours: '', feeDzd: '0' });
  const [driverIds, setDriverIds] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});

  const refresh = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      if (isDelivery) {
        const data = await apiRequest<{ orders: any[] }>('/api/delivery/orders', session.token);
        setOrders(data.orders);
      } else {
        const data = await apiRequest<{ orders: any[] }>(
          isAdmin ? '/api/admin/orders' : '/api/kitchen/orders',
          session.token
        );
        setOrders(data.orders);
      }
      if (isAdmin) {
        const [foodData, zoneData, dishData, settingsData, staffData, customersData, auditData] = await Promise.all([
          apiRequest<{ foods: any[] }>('/api/admin/foods', session.token),
          apiRequest<{ zones: any[] }>('/api/admin/zones', session.token),
          apiRequest<{ dishes: any[] }>('/api/admin/dishes', session.token),
          apiRequest<{ settings: any }>('/api/admin/settings', session.token),
          apiRequest<{ staff: any[] }>('/api/admin/staff', session.token),
          apiRequest<{ customers: any[] }>('/api/admin/customers', session.token),
          apiRequest<{ logs: any[] }>('/api/admin/audit', session.token),
        ]);
        setFoods(foodData.foods);
        setZones(zoneData.zones);
        setDishes(dishData.dishes);
        setStaff(staffData.staff);
        setCustomers(customersData.customers);
        setAuditLogs(auditData.logs);
        if (settingsData.settings) setSettings(settingsData.settings);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'تعذر تحميل بيانات العمليات.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    void refresh();
  }, [session.token]);

  const kitchenPrepSummary = useMemo(() => {
    if (isDelivery) return [];
    const map = new Map<string, { name: string; basis: string; source: string; grams: number }>();
    for (const order of orders) {
      if (isAdmin && order.order_status !== 'confirmed') continue;
      if (!Array.isArray(order.items)) continue;
      for (const item of order.items) {
        if (!Array.isArray(item.ingredients)) continue;
        for (const ing of item.ingredients) {
          const basis = ing.basis || 'unspecified';
          const source = ing.source || '';
          const key = `${ing.name}__${basis}__${source}`;
          const prev = map.get(key) ?? { name: ing.name, basis, source, grams: 0 };
          prev.grams += Number(ing.grams || 0);
          map.set(key, prev);
        }
      }
    }
    return Array.from(map.values()).sort((a, b) => b.grams - a.grams);
  }, [orders, isAdmin, isDelivery]);

  const run = async (task: () => Promise<void>, success: string) => {
    setBusy(true);
    setError('');
    setNotice('');
    try {
      await task();
      setNotice(success);
      await refresh();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'تعذر إكمال العملية.');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const createFood = () =>
    run(async () => {
      await apiRequest('/api/admin/foods', session.token, {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify({
          ...food,
          kcal: Number(food.kcal),
          protein: Number(food.protein),
          carbs: Number(food.carbs),
          fat: Number(food.fat),
          fiber: Number(food.fiber),
          priceDzdPer100: Number(food.priceDzdPer100),
          ediblePortionPct: food.ediblePortionPct === '' ? null : Number(food.ediblePortionPct),
          cookingYield: food.cookingYield === '' ? null : Number(food.cookingYield),
        }),
      });
      setFood({
        name: '',
        nameFr: '',
        nameEn: '',
        category: 'protein',
        kcal: '',
        protein: '',
        carbs: '',
        fat: '',
        fiber: '0',
        priceDzdPer100: '',
        nutritionBasis: 'raw',
        nutritionSource: '',
        sourceRecordId: '',
        ediblePortionPct: '',
        cookingYield: '',
        allergens: [],
      });
    }, 'حُفظ المكوّن كمسودة غير موثقة وغير نشطة.');

  const verifyFood = (item: any, verified: boolean) =>
    run(async () => {
      await apiRequest('/api/admin/foods/' + item.id + '/verification', session.token, {
        method: 'PATCH',
        body: JSON.stringify({
          verified,
          nutritionBasis: item.nutrition_basis,
          nutritionSource: item.nutrition_source ?? '',
          sourceRecordId: item.source_record_id ?? '',
        }),
      });
    }, verified ? 'تم اعتماد سجل التوثيق.' : 'تم إلغاء اعتماد التوثيق وإيقاف المكوّن.');

  const toggleFood = (item: any) =>
    run(async () => {
      await apiRequest('/api/admin/foods/' + item.id + '/active', session.token, {
        method: 'PATCH',
        body: JSON.stringify({ active: !item.active }),
      });
    }, 'تم تحديث حالة المكوّن.');

  const addDishIngredient = () => {
    if (!dishFoodId || Number(dishGrams) <= 0) return;
    setDishIngredients((prev) => [...prev, { foodId: dishFoodId, grams: Number(dishGrams) }]);
  };

  const createDish = () =>
    run(async () => {
      await apiRequest('/api/admin/dishes', session.token, {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify({ name: dishName, ingredients: dishIngredients }),
      });
      setDishName('');
      setDishIngredients([]);
    }, 'أُضيف الطبق كمسودة غير نشطة.');

  const toggleDish = (dish: any) =>
    run(async () => {
      await apiRequest('/api/admin/dishes/' + dish.id + '/active', session.token, {
        method: 'PATCH',
        body: JSON.stringify({ active: !dish.active }),
      });
    }, 'تم تحديث حالة الطبق.');

  const createZone = () =>
    run(async () => {
      await apiRequest('/api/admin/zones', session.token, {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify({ ...zone, feeDzd: Number(zone.feeDzd) }),
      });
      setZone({ title: '', areas: '', serviceHours: '', feeDzd: '0' });
    }, 'أُضيف نطاق الخدمة بحالة متوقفة.');

  const toggleZone = (item: any) =>
    run(async () => {
      await apiRequest('/api/admin/zones/' + item.id, session.token, {
        method: 'PATCH',
        body: JSON.stringify({ active: !item.active }),
      });
    }, 'تم تحديث نطاق الخدمة.');

  const saveSettings = () =>
    run(async () => {
      await apiRequest('/api/admin/settings', session.token, {
        method: 'PATCH',
        body: JSON.stringify({
          handlingPct: Number(settings.handling_pct),
          serviceFeePerMeal: Number(settings.service_fee_per_meal),
          cutoffHour: Number(settings.cutoff_hour),
          dailyCapacityBoxes: Number(settings.daily_capacity_boxes),
        }),
      });
    }, 'حُفظت إعدادات التشغيل.');

  const updateStage = (order: any, stage: string) =>
    run(async () => {
      await apiRequest('/api/kitchen/orders/' + order.id + '/stage', session.token, {
        method: 'PATCH',
        body: JSON.stringify({ stage }),
      });
    }, 'تم تحديث مرحلة الطلب.');

  const assignDriver = (order: any) =>
    run(async () => {
      await apiRequest('/api/admin/orders/' + order.id + '/assign-delivery', session.token, {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify({ deliveryUserId: driverIds[order.id] }),
      });
    }, 'تم إسناد الطلب إلى حساب التوصيل.');

  const confirmWaitlist = (order: any) =>
    run(async () => {
      await apiRequest('/api/admin/orders/' + order.id + '/confirm', session.token, { method: 'POST' });
    }, 'تم تأكيد الطلب بعد التحقق من السعة.');

  const cancelAdminOrder = (order: any) =>
    run(async () => {
      await apiRequest('/api/admin/orders/' + order.id + '/cancel', session.token, {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify({ reason: 'إلغاء إداري' }),
      });
    }, 'تم إلغاء الطلب.');

  const updateUserRole = (userId: string, nextRole: string) =>
    run(async () => {
      await apiRequest('/api/admin/staff/' + userId + '/role', session.token, {
        method: 'PATCH',
        body: JSON.stringify({ role: nextRole }),
      });
    }, 'تم تحديث صلاحية الحساب.');

  const field = (label: string, value: string, onChange: (value: string) => void, type = 'text', placeholder = '') => (
    <label className="block space-y-1">
      <span className="text-[11px] text-[#aaa79b]">{label}</span>
      <input
        value={value}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
        type={type}
        placeholder={placeholder}
        className="w-full rounded-lg border border-[#34372e] bg-[#0c100d] px-3 py-2.5 text-xs text-[#f4f1e9] outline-none focus:border-[#c9a85c]"
      />
    </label>
  );

  return (
    <main dir="rtl" className="min-h-screen bg-[#0b0e0c] text-[#f4f1e9] px-3 py-5 sm:px-6">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl space-y-5">
        <header className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-[#3b3a2d] bg-[#121612] p-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-[#c9a85c]/10 text-[#d8c18b]">
              {isDelivery ? <Truck size={19} /> : <ChefHat size={19} />}
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="text-[10px] tracking-[.2em] text-[#c9b783]">NUTRIFIT · OPERATIONS</div>
              <h1 className="mt-1 text-lg font-bold">
                {isAdmin ? 'مركز إدارة التشغيل' : isDelivery ? 'مسار التسليم' : 'لوحة إنتاج المطبخ'}
              </h1>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <button onClick={() => void refresh()} className="rounded-lg border border-[#34372e] p-2 text-[#c9b783] cursor-pointer" aria-label="تحديث">
              <RefreshCw size={16} />
            </button>
            <button onClick={onLogout} className="rounded-lg border border-[#34372e] px-3 py-2 text-xs text-[#c9b783] cursor-pointer">
              تسجيل الخروج
            </button>
          </div>
        </header>

        {isAdmin && (
          <nav className="flex gap-1 overflow-x-auto rounded-xl border border-[#292d25] bg-[#121612] p-1">
            {(['orders', 'foods', 'dishes', 'zones', 'settings', 'users', 'audit'] as Tab[]).map((key) => (
              <button
                key={key}
                onClick={() => setTab(key)}
                className={
                  'shrink-0 rounded-lg px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold cursor-pointer ' +
                  (tab === key ? 'bg-[#2b2619] text-[#e5cf94]' : 'text-[#929084]')
                }
              >
                {
                  (
                    {
                      orders: 'الطلبات والمطبخ',
                      foods: 'قاعدة الأغذية',
                      dishes: 'وصفات الشيف',
                      zones: 'مناطق الخدمة',
                      settings: 'التشغيل والأسعار',
                      users: 'العملاء والموظفون',
                      audit: 'سجل التدقيق',
                    } as Record<Tab, string>
                  )[key]
                }
              </button>
            ))}
          </nav>
        )}

        {error && (
          <div role="alert" className="rounded-xl border border-[#a64d42]/50 bg-[#281714] p-3 text-xs leading-5 text-[#efb9ad]">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}
        {notice && (
          <div role="status" className="rounded-xl border border-[#517b60]/50 bg-[#142219] p-3 text-xs leading-5 text-[#b8d4bd]">
            {notice}
          </div>
        )}
        {loading && <div className="py-10 text-center text-sm text-[#c9b783]">جارٍ تحميل بيانات التشغيل…</div>}

        {!loading && (tab === 'orders' || !isAdmin) && (
          <section className="space-y-4">
            {!isDelivery && kitchenPrepSummary.length > 0 && (
              <div className="rounded-2xl border border-[#c9a85c]/35 bg-[#121612] p-4 space-y-2.5">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                  <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-[#e5cf94]">ملخص المكونات الإجمالي للتحضير (الطلبات المؤكدة)</h2>
                  <span className="text-[11px] text-[#aaa79b] tabular-nums">{kitchenPrepSummary.length} صنف</span>
                </div>
                <div className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
                  {kitchenPrepSummary.map((row, idx) => (
                    <div key={idx} className="rounded-xl border border-[#292d25] bg-[#0c100d] p-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs">
                      <div className="min-w-0">
                        <div className="font-bold text-[#f4f1e9] truncate">{row.name}</div>
                        <div className="text-[10px] text-[#88877b] truncate">
                          {basisLabels[row.basis] ?? row.basis}
                          {row.source ? ` · ${row.source}` : ''}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <span className="font-bold text-[#e5cf94] tabular-nums shrink-0">{Math.round(row.grams)} غ</span>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <h2 className="text-sm font-bold">قائمة العمل · {orders.length} طلب</h2>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-[#88877b]">بيانات الخادم</span>
            </div>
            {orders.length === 0 && (
              <div className="rounded-xl border border-[#292d25] bg-[#121612] p-6 text-center text-xs text-[#88877b]">
                لا توجد طلبات مخصصة لهذا اليوم أو لهذا الحساب.
              </div>
            )}
            {orders.map((order) => (
              <article key={order.id} className="rounded-2xl border border-[#303329] bg-[#121612] p-4 space-y-3">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                  <div>
                    <div className="text-sm font-bold text-[#e5cf94]">{order.order_number}</div>
                    <div className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-[#aaa79b]">
                      {order.customer_name} · {order.delivery_zone_title}
                      {order.meal_count ? ` · ${order.meal_count} وجبات` : ''}
                      {order.delivery_date ? ` · ${String(order.delivery_date).slice(0, 10)}` : ''}
                      {order.total_dzd !== undefined ? ` · ${Number(order.total_dzd)} دج` : ''}
                      {order.delivery_user_name ? ` · الموصّل: ${order.delivery_user_name}` : ''}
                    </div>
                    {order.customer_note && (
                      <div className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-[#d4a24c]">ملاحظة العميل: {order.customer_note}</div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    {isDelivery && order.total_dzd !== undefined && (
                      <span className="rounded-full border border-[#5aa882]/40 bg-[#142219] px-3 py-1 text-[10px] font-bold text-[#b8d4bd] tabular-nums">
                        تحصيل: {Number(order.total_dzd)} دج ({order.payment_status === 'paid' ? 'مدفوع' : 'عند الاستلام'})
                      </span>
                    )}
                    <span className="rounded-full border border-[#c9a85c]/30 px-3 py-1 text-[10px] text-[#d8c18b]">
                      {order.order_status === 'cancelled'
                        ? 'ملغى'
                        : stageLabels[order.production_stage ?? 'queued'] ?? order.order_status}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>
                {Array.isArray(order.items) && (
                  <div className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2">
                    {order.items.map((item: any, index: number) => (
                      <div key={index} className="rounded-lg bg-[#0c100d] p-3 text-xs">
                        <div className="font-bold">{item.label}</div>
                        <div className="mt-1 text-[#aaa79b] tabular-nums">
                          {item.slot_name} · {Math.round(Number(item.grams))}غ
                        </div>
                        {item.ingredients?.length > 0 && (
                          <div className="mt-1 text-[10px] leading-5 text-[#aaa79b]">
                            {item.ingredients
                              .map(
                                (x: any) =>
                                  `${x.name} ${Math.round(Number(x.grams))}غ · ${basisLabels[x.basis] ?? 'حالة غير معروفة'}${x.source ? ` · ${x.source}` : ''}`
                              )
                              .join(' | ')}
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
                {!isDelivery && order.production_stage && order.order_status === 'confirmed' && (
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 border-t border-[#292d25] pt-3">
                    <label className="text-[11px] text-[#aaa79b]">تحديث المرحلة</label>
                    <select
                      value={order.production_stage}
                      onChange={(e) => void updateStage(order, e.target.value)}
                      disabled={busy}
                      className="min-w-0 flex-1 rounded-lg border border-[#34372e] bg-[#0c100d] px-3 py-2 text-xs"
                    >
                      {(isAdmin
                        ? ['queued', 'preparing', 'packed', 'out_for_delivery', 'delivered']
                        : ['queued', 'preparing', 'packed']
                      ).map((stage) => (
                        <option key={stage} value={stage}>
                          {stageLabels[stage]}
                        </option>
                      ))}
                    </select>
                  </div>
                )}
                {isDelivery && (
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-t border-[#292d25] pt-3 text-xs">
                    <span className="text-[#aaa79b]">
                      {order.production_stage === 'packed'
                        ? 'استلام الطلب من المطبخ'
                        : order.production_stage === 'out_for_delivery'
                          ? 'تسليم الطلب للعميل'
                          : 'بانتظار تجهيز المطبخ'}
                    </span>
                    {order.production_stage === 'packed' && (
                      <button
                        onClick={() => void updateStage(order, 'out_for_delivery')}
                        disabled={busy}
                        className="rounded-lg bg-[#c9a85c] px-4 py-2 font-bold text-[#17140d] disabled:opacity-40 cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        بدء التوصيل
                      </button>
                    )}
                    {order.production_stage === 'out_for_delivery' && (
                      <button
                        onClick={() => void updateStage(order, 'delivered')}
                        disabled={busy}
                        className="rounded-lg bg-[#c9a85c] px-4 py-2 font-bold text-[#17140d] disabled:opacity-40 cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        تأكيد التسليم
                      </button>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}
                {isAdmin && order.order_status !== 'cancelled' && (
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 border-t border-[#292d25] pt-3">
                    {order.order_status === 'waitlisted' && (
                      <button
                        onClick={() => void confirmWaitlist(order)}
                        disabled={busy}
                        className="rounded-lg bg-[#c9a85c] px-3 py-2 text-[11px] font-bold text-[#17140d] disabled:opacity-40 cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        تأكيد من قائمة الانتظار
                      </button>
                    )}
                    {order.order_status === 'confirmed' && (
                      <>
                        <select
                          value={driverIds[order.id] ?? order.delivery_user_id ?? ''}
                          onChange={(e) => setDriverIds((prev) => ({ ...prev, [order.id]: e.target.value }))}
                          className="min-w-0 flex-1 rounded-lg border border-[#34372e] bg-[#0c100d] px-3 py-2 text-[11px]"
                        >
                          <option value="">اختر الموصّل</option>
                          {staff
                            .filter((person) => person.role === 'delivery')
                            .map((person) => (
                              <option key={person.id} value={person.id}>
                                {person.name} · {person.email}
                              </option>
                            ))}
                        </select>
                        <button
                          onClick={() => void assignDriver(order)}
                          disabled={busy || !driverIds[order.id]}
                          className="rounded-lg border border-[#c9a85c]/40 px-3 py-2 text-[11px] text-[#e5cf94] disabled:opacity-40 cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          إسناد التوصيل
                        </button>
                      </>
                    )}
                    <button
                      onClick={() => void cancelAdminOrder(order)}
                      disabled={busy}
                      className="rounded-lg border border-[#a64d42]/50 bg-[#281714] px-3 py-2 text-[11px] text-[#efb9ad] disabled:opacity-40 cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      إلغاء الطلب
                    </button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </article>
            ))}
          </section>
        )}

        {!loading && isAdmin && tab === 'foods' && (
          <section className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_1.2fr]">
            <form
              onSubmit={(e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                void createFood();
              }}
              className="rounded-2xl border border-[#303329] bg-[#121612] p-4 space-y-3"
            >
              <h2 className="text-sm font-bold">إضافة مكوّن غذائي</h2>
              {field('اسم المكوّن (بالعربية)', food.name, (v) => setFood({ ...food, name: v }))}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                {field('الاسم بالفرنسية (اختياري)', food.nameFr, (v) => setFood({ ...food, nameFr: v }))}
                {field('الاسم بالإنجليزية (اختياري)', food.nameEn, (v) => setFood({ ...food, nameEn: v }))}
              </div>
              <label className="block text-[11px] text-[#aaa79b]">
                التصنيف
                <select
                  value={food.category}
                  onChange={(e) => setFood({ ...food, category: e.target.value })}
                  className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-[#34372e] bg-[#0c100d] p-2.5 text-xs"
                >
                  <option value="protein">بروتين</option>
                  <option value="carb">كربوهيدرات</option>
                  <option value="fat">دهون</option>
                  <option value="veg">خضروات</option>
                  <option value="juice">مشروبات</option>
                </select>
              </label>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                {field('kcal / 100غ', food.kcal, (v) => setFood({ ...food, kcal: v }), 'number')}
                {field('بروتين غ', food.protein, (v) => setFood({ ...food, protein: v }), 'number')}
                {field('كربوهيدرات غ', food.carbs, (v) => setFood({ ...food, carbs: v }), 'number')}
                {field('دهون غ', food.fat, (v) => setFood({ ...food, fat: v }), 'number')}
                {field('ألياف غ', food.fiber, (v) => setFood({ ...food, fiber: v }), 'number')}
                {field('السعر دج / 100غ', food.priceDzdPer100, (v) => setFood({ ...food, priceDzdPer100: v }), 'number')}
                {field('النسبة القابلة للأكل %', food.ediblePortionPct, (v) => setFood({ ...food, ediblePortionPct: v }), 'number', '100')}
                {field('معامل مردود الطهي', food.cookingYield, (v) => setFood({ ...food, cookingYield: v }), 'number', '0.75')}
              </div>
              <label className="block text-[11px] text-[#aaa79b]">
                حالة الوزن
                <select
                  value={food.nutritionBasis}
                  onChange={(e) => setFood({ ...food, nutritionBasis: e.target.value })}
                  className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-[#34372e] bg-[#0c100d] p-2.5 text-xs"
                >
                  <option value="raw">نيء</option>
                  <option value="cooked">مطبوخ</option>
                  <option value="as_sold">كما يباع</option>
                  <option value="unspecified">غير محدد</option>
                </select>
              </label>
              {field('المصدر العلمي', food.nutritionSource, (v) => setFood({ ...food, nutritionSource: v }), 'text', 'USDA FoodData Central')}
              {field('معرّف المصدر (إن توفر)', food.sourceRecordId, (v) => setFood({ ...food, sourceRecordId: v }), 'text', 'FDC 171077')}
              <div className="space-y-2">
                <div className="text-[11px] text-[#aaa79b]">مسببات الحساسية الموجودة</div>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3 text-[11px]">
                  {(['gluten', 'eggs', 'fish', 'milk', 'nuts'] as string[]).map((key) => (
                    <label key={key} className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      <input
                        type="checkbox"
                        checked={food.allergens.includes(key)}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          setFood({
                            ...food,
                            allergens: e.target.checked
                              ? [...food.allergens, key]
                              : food.allergens.filter((x: string) => x !== key),
                          })
                        }
                      />
                      {({ gluten: 'غلوتين', eggs: 'بيض', fish: 'سمك', milk: 'حليب', nuts: 'مكسرات' } as Record<string, string>)[key]}
                    </label>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
              <button disabled={busy} className="w-full rounded-xl bg-[#c9a85c] py-3 text-xs font-extrabold text-[#17140d] disabled:opacity-40 cursor-pointer">
                <Plus className="ml-1 inline h-4 w-4" />
                حفظ كمسودة غير موثقة
              </button>
            </form>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h2 className="text-sm font-bold">سجل المكوّنات · {foods.length}</h2>
              {foods.map((item) => (
                <div key={item.id} className="rounded-xl border border-[#303329] bg-[#121612] p-3 space-y-2">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                    <div>
                      <div className="text-xs font-bold">
                        {item.name_ar}
                        {(item.name_fr || item.name_en) && (
                          <span className="text-[10px] font-normal text-[#88877b]">
                            {' '}
                            ({[item.name_fr, item.name_en].filter(Boolean).join(' / ')})
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </div>
                      <div className="mt-1 text-[10px] text-[#88877b]">
                        {item.category} · {basisLabels[item.nutrition_basis] ?? item.nutrition_basis} · {item.nutrition_verified ? 'موثق' : 'غير موثق'} · {item.active ? 'نشط' : 'متوقف'}
                        {item.nutrition_source ? ` · ${item.nutrition_source}` : ''}
                        {item.source_record_id ? ` (#${item.source_record_id})` : ''}
                        {item.cooking_yield ? ` · مردود الطهي ${item.cooking_yield}` : ''}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-left text-[10px] text-[#c9b783] tabular-nums">{item.price_dzd_per_100} دج/100غ</div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                    {!item.nutrition_verified && (
                      <button
                        disabled={busy || !item.nutrition_source || item.nutrition_basis === 'unspecified'}
                        onClick={() => void verifyFood(item, true)}
                        className="rounded-lg border border-[#c9a85c]/40 px-3 py-2 text-[10px] text-[#e5cf94] disabled:opacity-40 cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        اعتماد التوثيق
                      </button>
                    )}
                    {item.nutrition_verified && (
                      <>
                        <button
                          disabled={busy}
                          onClick={() => void toggleFood(item)}
                          className="rounded-lg border border-[#5c9270]/40 px-3 py-2 text-[10px] text-[#a9d0b2] cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          {item.active ? 'إيقاف' : 'تفعيل'}
                        </button>
                        <button
                          disabled={busy}
                          onClick={() => void verifyFood(item, false)}
                          className="rounded-lg border border-[#a64d42]/40 px-3 py-2 text-[10px] text-[#efb9ad] cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          إلغاء الاعتماد
                        </button>
                      </>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </section>
        )}

        {!loading && isAdmin && tab === 'dishes' && (
          <section className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-2">
            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-[#303329] bg-[#121612] p-4 space-y-3">
              <h2 className="text-sm font-bold">بناء وصفة</h2>
              {field('اسم الطبق', dishName, setDishName)}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-[1fr_100px_auto] items-end gap-2">
                <label className="block text-[11px] text-[#aaa79b]">
                  المكوّن
                  <select value={dishFoodId} onChange={(e) => setDishFoodId(e.target.value)} className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-[#34372e] bg-[#0c100d] p-2.5 text-xs">
                    <option value="">اختر مكوّناً نشطاً</option>
                    {foods
                      .filter((x) => x.active && x.nutrition_verified)
                      .map((x) => (
                        <option key={x.id} value={x.id}>
                          {x.name_ar}
                        </option>
                      ))}
                  </select>
                </label>
                {field('الوزن غ', dishGrams, setDishGrams, 'number')}
                <button type="button" onClick={addDishIngredient} className="rounded-lg border border-[#c9a85c]/40 p-2.5 text-[#e5cf94] cursor-pointer">
                  <Plus size={15} />
                </button>
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-1">
                {dishIngredients.map((item, index) => (
                  <div key={index} className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg bg-[#0c100d] p-2 text-[11px]">
                    <span>
                      {foods.find((x) => x.id === item.foodId)?.name_ar ?? 'مكوّن'} · {item.grams}غ
                    </span>
                    <button type="button" onClick={() => setDishIngredients((prev) => prev.filter((_, i) => i !== index))} className="text-[#d48d7f] cursor-pointer">
                      حذف
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => void createDish()}
                disabled={busy || !dishName.trim() || dishIngredients.length === 0}
                className="w-full rounded-xl bg-[#c9a85c] py-3 text-xs font-bold text-[#17140d] disabled:opacity-40 cursor-pointer"
              >
                حفظ الوصفة كمسودة
              </button>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h2 className="text-sm font-bold">الوصفات المسجلة · {dishes.length}</h2>
              {dishes.map((dish) => (
                <div key={dish.id} className="rounded-xl border border-[#303329] bg-[#121612] p-3">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold">{dish.name_ar}</span>
                    <button onClick={() => void toggleDish(dish)} disabled={busy} className="text-[10px] text-[#e5cf94] cursor-pointer">
                      {dish.active ? 'إيقاف' : 'تفعيل بعد المراجعة'}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                  <div className="mt-2 text-[10px] text-[#88877b]">{(dish.ingredients ?? []).map((x: any) => x.name + ' ' + x.grams + 'غ').join(' · ')}</div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </section>
        )}

        {!loading && isAdmin && tab === 'zones' && (
          <section className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-2">
            <form
              onSubmit={(e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                void createZone();
              }}
              className="rounded-2xl border border-[#303329] bg-[#121612] p-4 space-y-3"
            >
              <h2 className="text-sm font-bold">إضافة منطقة خدمة</h2>
              {field('اسم المنطقة', zone.title, (v) => setZone({ ...zone, title: v }))}
              {field('الأحياء المشمولة', zone.areas, (v) => setZone({ ...zone, areas: v }))}
              {field('ساعات الخدمة الفعلية', zone.serviceHours, (v) => setZone({ ...zone, serviceHours: v }))}
              {field('رسوم التوصيل دج', zone.feeDzd, (v) => setZone({ ...zone, feeDzd: v }), 'number')}
              <button disabled={busy} className="w-full rounded-xl bg-[#c9a85c] py-3 text-xs font-bold text-[#17140d] cursor-pointer">
                إضافة كنطاق متوقف
              </button>
            </form>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h2 className="text-sm font-bold">نطاقات الخدمة · {zones.length}</h2>
              {zones.map((item) => (
                <div key={item.id} className="rounded-xl border border-[#303329] bg-[#121612] p-3">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold">{item.title}</span>
                    <button onClick={() => void toggleZone(item)} disabled={busy} className="text-[10px] text-[#e5cf94] cursor-pointer">
                      {item.active ? 'إيقاف' : 'تفعيل'}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                  <div className="mt-1 text-[10px] text-[#88877b]">
                    {item.areas} · {item.service_hours} · {item.fee_dzd} دج
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </section>
        )}

        {!loading && isAdmin && tab === 'settings' && (
          <section className="max-w-xl rounded-2xl border border-[#303329] bg-[#121612] p-4 space-y-3">
            <h2 className="text-sm font-bold">إعدادات التسعير والطاقة اليومية</h2>
            {field('المناولة %', String(settings.handling_pct), (v) => setSettings({ ...settings, handling_pct: v }), 'number')}
            {field('أجر الخدمة لكل وجبة دج', String(settings.service_fee_per_meal), (v) => setSettings({ ...settings, service_fee_per_meal: v }), 'number')}
            {field('موعد الإغلاق بالساعة 0–23', String(settings.cutoff_hour), (v) => setSettings({ ...settings, cutoff_hour: v }), 'number')}
            {field('سعة العلب اليومية (0 = إيقاف الطلبات)', String(settings.daily_capacity_boxes), (v) => setSettings({ ...settings, daily_capacity_boxes: v }), 'number')}
            <button onClick={() => void saveSettings()} disabled={busy} className="w-full rounded-xl bg-[#c9a85c] py-3 text-xs font-bold text-[#17140d] cursor-pointer">
              حفظ الإعدادات
            </button>
          </section>
        )}

        {!loading && isAdmin && tab === 'users' && (
          <section className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-2">
            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-[#303329] bg-[#121612] p-4 space-y-3">
              <h2 className="text-sm font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
                <Users size={16} className="text-[#c9a85c]" />
                فريق التشغيل ({staff.length})
              </h2>
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {staff.map((person) => (
                  <div key={person.id} className="rounded-xl border border-[#292d25] bg-[#0c100d] p-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs">
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className="font-bold text-[#f4f1e9] truncate">{person.name}</div>
                      <div className="text-[10px] text-[#88877b] truncate">{person.email}</div>
                    </div>
                    <select
                      value={person.role}
                      disabled={busy || person.id === session.user.id}
                      onChange={(e) => void updateUserRole(person.id, e.target.value)}
                      className="rounded-lg border border-[#34372e] bg-[#121612] px-2.5 py-1.5 text-[11px] text-[#e5cf94] disabled:opacity-50"
                    >
                      {(['admin', 'kitchen', 'delivery', 'customer'] as string[]).map((r) => (
                        <option key={r} value={r}>
                          {roleLabels[r]}
                        </option>
                      ))}
                    </select>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-[#303329] bg-[#121612] p-4 space-y-3">
              <h2 className="text-sm font-bold">العملاء المسجلون ({customers.length})</h2>
              <div className="space-y-2 max-h-[60vh] overflow-y-auto">
                {customers.map((cust) => (
                  <div key={cust.id} className="rounded-xl border border-[#292d25] bg-[#0c100d] p-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs">
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className="font-bold text-[#f4f1e9] truncate">{cust.name}</div>
                      <div className="text-[10px] text-[#88877b] truncate">
                        {cust.email} · {cust.orders_count ?? 0} طلبات
                        {cust.goal ? ` · هدف: ${cust.goal}` : ''}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <select
                      value={cust.role}
                      disabled={busy}
                      onChange={(e) => void updateUserRole(cust.id, e.target.value)}
                      className="rounded-lg border border-[#34372e] bg-[#121612] px-2.5 py-1.5 text-[11px] text-[#aaa79b]"
                    >
                      {(['customer', 'kitchen', 'delivery', 'admin'] as string[]).map((r) => (
                        <option key={r} value={r}>
                          {roleLabels[r]}
                        </option>
                      ))}
                    </select>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </section>
        )}

        {!loading && isAdmin && tab === 'audit' && (
          <section className="rounded-2xl border border-[#303329] bg-[#121612] p-4 space-y-3">
            <h2 className="text-sm font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
              <History size={16} className="text-[#c9a85c]" />
              سجل التدقيق الأمني والتشغيلي ({auditLogs.length})
            </h2>
            {auditLogs.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="text-xs text-[#88877b]">لا توجد سجلات بعد.</div>
            ) : (
              <div className="divide-y divide-[#222520] max-h-[65vh] overflow-y-auto text-xs">
                {auditLogs.map((log) => (
                  <div key={log.id} className="py-2.5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                    <div>
                      <span className="font-bold text-[#e5cf94]">{log.action}</span>{' '}
                      <span className="text-[#aaa79b]">
                        ({log.entity_type} · {String(log.entity_id).slice(0, 8)})
                      </span>
                      <div className="text-[10px] text-[#88877b] mt-0.5">
                        بواسطة: {log.actor_name || log.actor_email || 'النظام'}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-[10px] text-[#88877b] tabular-nums">
                      {new Date(log.created_at).toLocaleString('ar-DZ')}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </section>
        )}

        <footer className="border-t border-[#292d25] pt-4 text-[10px] leading-5 text-[#77786e]">
          <ShieldCheck className="ml-1 inline h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          كل إجراء يُرسل إلى الخادم ويُسجل في سجل التدقيق. لا تُفعّل غذاءً أو منطقة قبل مراجعة بياناتها واعتمادها.
        </footer>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
};


==========================================================================================
FILE: src/components/PremiumMealBuilder.tsx
==========================================================================================
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { AppState, AppTab, CartItem, FoodItem } from '../types';
import { CATEGORY_LABELS, getFoodImage } from '../utils/media';
import {
  calcCartTotals, calcGaps, calcNutrition, cartItemMacros, clamp,
  foodMacros, formatAllergenList, getBlockedAllergensForFood,
  getFoodGramDefaults, getMealSlotName, round0, round1,
} from '../utils/nutrition';
import { ArrowLeft, Check, Minus, Plus, Search, ShoppingBag, Trash2, X } from 'lucide-react';

interface Props {
  state: AppState;
  onUpdate: (updater: (prev: AppState) => AppState) => void;
  onNavigate: (tab: AppTab) => void;
  onToast: (msg: string) => void;
  onCartPulse?: () => void;
  pickerRequest?: { slot: number; seq: number } | null;
}
type Category = 'all' | 'protein' | 'carb' | 'fat' | 'veg';

const categories: { id: Category; label: string }[] = [
  { id: 'all', label: 'الكل' }, { id: 'protein', label: 'مصادر البروتين' },
  { id: 'carb', label: 'مصادر الطاقة' }, { id: 'fat', label: 'الدهون' },
  { id: 'veg', label: 'الخضروات' },
];

export const PremiumMealBuilder: React.FC<Props> = ({
  state, onUpdate, onNavigate, onToast, onCartPulse, pickerRequest,
}) => {
  const [slot, setSlot] = useState(() => clamp(pickerRequest?.slot ?? state.activeSlot ?? 0, 0, Math.max(0, state.n - 1)));
  const [category, setCategory] = useState<Category>('all');
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [grams, setGrams] = useState(150);
  const [plateUid, setPlateUid] = useState('new');

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!pickerRequest) return;
    const next = clamp(pickerRequest.slot, 0, Math.max(0, state.n - 1));
    setSlot(next); setSelectedId(null); setQuery(''); setCategory('all');
  }, [pickerRequest?.seq, state.n]);

  const nutrition = calcNutrition(state);
  const totals = calcCartTotals(state);
  const gaps = calcGaps(state);
  const activeItems = state.meals.filter(item => clamp(item.slot || 0, 0, state.n - 1) === slot);
  const selected = selectedId ? state.foods.find(food => food.id === selectedId) ?? null : null;
  const filtered = useMemo(() => state.foods.filter(food => {
    if (food.cat === 'juice' || (category !== 'all' && food.cat !== category)) return false;
    const needle = query.trim().toLocaleLowerCase();
    return !needle || [food.name, food.nameFr, food.nameEn].some(value => value?.toLocaleLowerCase().includes(needle));
  }), [state.foods, category, query]);

  if (nutrition.err) return <section className="rounded-3xl border border-[var(--nf-border)] bg-[var(--nf-surface)] p-6 text-sm leading-7 text-[var(--nf-bad)]">{nutrition.err}</section>;

  const choose = (food: FoodItem) => {
    const blocked = getBlockedAllergensForFood(food, state.al);
    if (food.available === false) { onToast('هذا المكوّن غير متاح حاليًا.'); return; }
    if (blocked.length) { onToast('مكوّن محجوب بسبب الحساسية: ' + formatAllergenList(blocked)); return; }
    setSelectedId(food.id);
    setGrams(getFoodGramDefaults(food).def);
    setPlateUid(activeItems.find(item => item.kind === 'custom')?.uid ?? 'new');
  };

  const addIngredient = () => {
    if (!selected || grams <= 0) return;
    const amount = Math.round(grams);
    onUpdate(prev => {
      const target = plateUid === 'new'
        ? undefined
        : prev.meals.find(item => item.uid === plateUid && item.kind === 'custom' && item.slot === slot);
      if (target) {
        const ingredients = [...(target.ing ?? [])];
        const index = ingredients.findIndex(([id]) => id === selected.id);
        if (index >= 0) ingredients[index] = [selected.id, ingredients[index][1] + amount];
        else ingredients.push([selected.id, amount]);
        return { ...prev, activeSlot: slot, meals: prev.meals.map(item => item.uid === target.uid
          ? { ...item, name: 'طبق مخصص', ing: ingredients } : item) };
      }
      const item: CartItem = {
        uid: 'plate_' + Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7),
        kind: 'custom', id: 'custom_mix', name: 'طبق مخصص',
        ing: [[selected.id, amount]], slot,
      };
      return { ...prev, activeSlot: slot, meals: [...prev.meals, item] };
    });
    onCartPulse?.();
    onToast('أُضيف المكوّن إلى ' + getMealSlotName(state, slot));
    setSelectedId(null);
  };

  const removeItem = (uid: string) => onUpdate(prev => ({ ...prev, meals: prev.meals.filter(item => item.uid !== uid) }));
  const addSlot = () => {
    if (state.n >= 5) return;
    onUpdate(prev => ({ ...prev, n: prev.n + 1, activeSlot: prev.n }));
    setSlot(state.n);
  };

  return <div className="space-y-6 pb-4">
    <header className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-4">
      <div>
        <div className="text-[10px] font-bold tracking-[.18em] text-[var(--nf-brass)]">THE MEAL STUDIO · BUILD YOUR DAY</div>
        <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-[var(--nf-ink)] sm:text-4xl">صمّم وجبات يومك</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 max-w-2xl text-sm leading-7 text-[var(--nf-muted)]">ابدأ باختيار الوجبة، ثم أضف المكونات وحدد وزن كل مكوّن. سترى أثر اختياراتك على خطتك قبل إضافتها.</p>
      </div>
      <button type="button" onClick={() => onNavigate('cart')} className="inline-flex min-h-11 items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-[var(--nf-border)] bg-[var(--nf-surface)] px-4 text-xs font-bold text-[var(--nf-green)]">
        <ShoppingBag size={16}/> مراجعة الخطة <span className="rounded-full bg-[var(--nf-green-soft)] px-2 py-1 tabular-nums">{state.meals.length}</span>
      </button>
    </header>

    <section className="rounded-[26px] bg-[var(--nf-green)] p-5 text-white sm:p-6">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div><div className="text-[10px] font-semibold tracking-[.16em] text-white/60">YOUR DAILY TARGET</div><div className="mt-1 text-sm font-semibold">ملخص خطتك الغذائية</div></div>
        <div className="text-left"><div className="text-[10px] text-white/60">الطاقة المخططة</div><div className="text-2xl font-semibold tabular-nums">{round0(totals.kcal)} <span className="text-xs font-normal">/ {round0(nutrition.kcal)} kcal</span></div></div>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-4 h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-white/15"><div className="h-full rounded-full bg-[#d9c5a2] transition-[width] duration-300" style={{width: `${Math.min(100, totals.kcal / Math.max(1, nutrition.kcal) * 100)}%`}}/></div>
      <div className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-4">
        {[['بروتين', totals.p, nutrition.fp, 'غ'], ['كربوهيدرات', totals.c, nutrition.C, 'غ'], ['دهون', totals.f, nutrition.F, 'غ'], ['المتبقي', gaps.kcal, 0, 'kcal']].map(([label, value, target, unit]) =>
          <div key={String(label)} className="rounded-xl border border-white/15 bg-white/5 p-3">
            <div className="text-[10px] text-white/60">{label}</div>
            <div className="mt-1 text-lg font-semibold tabular-nums">{round0(Number(value))}<span className="mr-1 text-[10px] font-normal">{unit}</span></div>
            {Number(target) > 0 && <div className="text-[9px] text-white/55">الهدف {round0(Number(target))}</div>}
          </div>)}
      </div>
    </section>

    <section className="space-y-3">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3"><div><h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-[var(--nf-ink)]">1. اختر الوجبة</h2><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--nf-muted)]">يمكنك التنقل بين الوجبات وإكمال كل واحدة على حدة.</p></div>
        {state.n < 5 && <button type="button" onClick={addSlot} className="min-h-10 rounded-xl border border-[var(--nf-border)] px-3 text-xs font-bold text-[var(--nf-green)]"><Plus size={14} className="ml-1 inline"/> وجبة جديدة</button>}
      </div>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-5">
        {Array.from({length: state.n}, (_, index) => {
          const items = state.meals.filter(item => item.slot === index);
          const selectedSlot = slot === index;
          const macros = items.reduce((sum, item) => { const m = cartItemMacros(item, state.foods, state.dishes); return {kcal:sum.kcal+m.kcal, p:sum.p+m.p}; }, {kcal:0,p:0});
          return <button key={index} type="button" onClick={() => {setSlot(index); onUpdate(prev => ({...prev, activeSlot:index}));}} className={`touch-press rounded-2xl border p-3 text-right transition-all ${selectedSlot ? 'border-[var(--nf-green)] bg-[var(--nf-green-soft)] shadow-sm' : 'border-[var(--nf-border)] bg-[var(--nf-surface)]'}`}>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between"><span className="text-[10px] text-[var(--nf-muted)]">0{index+1}</span>{items.length > 0 && <Check size={14} className="text-[var(--nf-green)]"/>}</div>
            <div className="mt-2 text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--nf-ink)]">{getMealSlotName(state,index)}</div>
            <div className="mt-1 text-[10px] text-[var(--nf-muted)] tabular-nums">{round0(macros.kcal)} kcal · P {round0(macros.p)}غ</div>
          </button>;
        })}
      </div>
    </section>

    <div className="grid items-start gap-5 xl:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1.2fr)_minmax(300px,.8fr)]">
      <section className="min-w-0 space-y-4">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3"><div><h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-[var(--nf-ink)]">2. اختر المكونات</h2><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--nf-muted)]">القيم المعروضة لكل 100 غرام، ما لم يُذكر غير ذلك.</p></div>
          <div className="relative w-full sm:w-64"><Search size={16} className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[var(--nf-muted)]"/><input value={query} onChange={e=>setQuery(e.target.value)} placeholder="ابحث عن مكوّن..." className="min-h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-[var(--nf-border)] bg-[var(--nf-surface)] pr-9 pl-3 text-xs outline-none focus:border-[var(--nf-green)]"/></div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-1">
          {categories.map(item => <button key={item.id} type="button" onClick={()=>setCategory(item.id)} className={`min-h-10 shrink-0 rounded-full border px-4 text-xs font-semibold ${category===item.id?'border-[var(--nf-green)] bg-[var(--nf-green)] text-white':'border-[var(--nf-border)] bg-[var(--nf-surface)] text-[var(--nf-muted)]'}`}>{item.label}</button>)}
        </div>
        <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
          {filtered.map(food => {
            const blocked = getBlockedAllergensForFood(food, state.al);
            const unavailable = food.available === false;
            return <article key={food.id} className="group overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-[var(--nf-border)] bg-[var(--nf-surface)] transition-all hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:shadow-lg">
              <button type="button" disabled={unavailable || blocked.length>0} onClick={()=>choose(food)} className="w-full text-right disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50">
                <div className="relative aspect-[16/9] overflow-hidden bg-[var(--nf-surface-2)]"><img src={getFoodImage(food)} alt={food.name} loading="lazy" className="h-full w-full object-cover transition-transform duration-500 group-hover:scale-105"/><span className="absolute right-3 top-3 rounded-full bg-white/90 px-2.5 py-1 text-[10px] font-semibold text-[var(--nf-ink)]">{CATEGORY_LABELS[food.cat]}</span></div>
                <div className="p-4">
                  <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2"><div><h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--nf-ink)]">{food.name}</h3><p className="mt-1 text-[10px] text-[var(--nf-muted)]">{food.nutritionDataStatus==='verified'?'بيانات غذائية موثقة':'بيانات قيد التحقق'} · {food.nutritionBasis==='raw'?'وزن نيء':food.nutritionBasis==='cooked'?'وزن مطبوخ':'أساس الوزن غير محدد'}</p></div><div className="text-left text-sm font-bold text-[var(--nf-green)] tabular-nums">{round1(food.price)} <span className="text-[10px] font-normal">دج</span><div className="text-[9px] font-normal text-[var(--nf-muted)]">/ 100غ</div></div></div>
                  <div className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-4 gap-1.5 border-t border-[var(--nf-border)] pt-3 text-center tabular-nums"><div><b className="block text-xs">{round0(food.per100.kcal)}</b><span className="text-[9px] text-[var(--nf-muted)]">kcal</span></div><div><b className="block text-xs">{round1(food.per100.p)}غ</b><span className="text-[9px] text-[var(--nf-muted)]">بروتين</span></div><div><b className="block text-xs">{round1(food.per100.c)}غ</b><span className="text-[9px] text-[var(--nf-muted)]">كارب</span></div><div><b className="block text-xs">{round1(food.per100.f)}غ</b><span className="text-[9px] text-[var(--nf-muted)]">دهون</span></div></div>
                  {blocked.length>0 && <p className="mt-2 text-[10px] text-[var(--nf-bad)]">محجوب بسبب الحساسية: {formatAllergenList(blocked)}</p>}
                </div>
              </button>
            </article>;
          })}
        </div>
        {filtered.length===0 && <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-[var(--nf-border)] p-8 text-center text-sm text-[var(--nf-muted)]">لا توجد نتائج. جرّب كلمة بحث أخرى أو تصنيفًا مختلفًا.</div>}
      </section>

      <aside className="space-y-4 xl:sticky xl:top-5">
        <section className="rounded-[24px] border border-[var(--nf-border)] bg-[var(--nf-surface)] p-4 sm:p-5">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between"><div><h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-[var(--nf-ink)]">3. تكوين {getMealSlotName(state,slot)}</h2><p className="mt-1 text-[10px] text-[var(--nf-muted)]">المكونات التي أضفتها إلى هذه الوجبة.</p></div><span className="rounded-full bg-[var(--nf-green-soft)] px-2.5 py-1 text-[10px] font-bold text-[var(--nf-green)]">{activeItems.length} عنصر</span></div>
          <div className="mt-4 divide-y divide-[var(--nf-border)]">
            {activeItems.map(item => { const m=cartItemMacros(item,state.foods,state.dishes); return <div key={item.uid} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 py-3"><div className="min-w-0"><div className="truncate text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--nf-ink)]">{item.name || state.foods.find(food=>food.id===item.id)?.name || 'طبق مخصص'}</div><div className="mt-1 text-[10px] text-[var(--nf-muted)] tabular-nums">{round0(m.grams)}غ · {round0(m.kcal)} kcal · P{round1(m.p)}غ · {round1(m.price)} دج</div>{item.kind==='custom' && <div className="mt-1 text-[9px] text-[var(--nf-muted)]">{(item.ing??[]).map(([id,g])=>`${state.foods.find(food=>food.id===id)?.name??id} ${g}غ`).join(' · ')}</div>}</div><button type="button" onClick={()=>removeItem(item.uid)} aria-label="حذف العنصر" className="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl text-[var(--nf-muted)] hover:bg-red-50 hover:text-[var(--nf-bad)]"><Trash2 size={15}/></button></div>;})}
          </div>
          {activeItems.length===0 && <p className="py-5 text-center text-xs leading-6 text-[var(--nf-muted)]">هذه الوجبة فارغة. اختر مكوّنًا من القائمة لإضافته.</p>}
          <div className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 border-t border-[var(--nf-border)] pt-4 text-xs"><div><span className="text-[var(--nf-muted)]">الطاقة</span><b className="mt-1 block tabular-nums">{round0(activeItems.reduce((s,i)=>s+cartItemMacros(i,state.foods,state.dishes).kcal,0))} kcal</b></div><div><span className="text-[var(--nf-muted)]">التكلفة</span><b className="mt-1 block tabular-nums">{round0(activeItems.reduce((s,i)=>s+cartItemMacros(i,state.foods,state.dishes).price,0))} دج</b></div></div>
          <button type="button" onClick={()=>onNavigate('cart')} disabled={state.meals.length===0} className="mt-4 flex min-h-12 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-[var(--nf-green)] px-4 text-xs font-bold text-white disabled:opacity-40"><ShoppingBag size={15}/> مراجعة السلة والخطة <ArrowLeft size={15}/></button>
        </section>
        <section className="rounded-[24px] border border-[var(--nf-border)] bg-[var(--nf-surface-2)] p-4 text-xs leading-6 text-[var(--nf-muted)]"><b className="text-[var(--nf-ink)]">منهجية الوزن</b><p className="mt-1">انتبه إلى أساس الوزن: نيء أو مطبوخ أو كما يباع. لا تقارن قيمًا غذائية محسوبة على أساسين مختلفين.</p></section>
      </aside>
    </div>

    {selected && (() => { const cfg=getFoodGramDefaults(selected); const m=foodMacros(selected,grams); return <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end justify-center bg-black/45 backdrop-blur-sm sm:items-center sm:p-4" onClick={e=>{if(e.target===e.currentTarget)setSelectedId(null);}}>
      <section role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label={`تحديد كمية ${selected.name}`} className="w-full max-w-xl overflow-hidden rounded-t-[28px] bg-[var(--nf-surface)] shadow-2xl sm:rounded-[28px]">
        <header className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-[var(--nf-border)] p-4"><div><div className="text-[10px] text-[var(--nf-muted)]">{getMealSlotName(state,slot)} · {CATEGORY_LABELS[selected.cat]}</div><h2 className="mt-1 text-lg font-semibold text-[var(--nf-ink)]">{selected.name}</h2></div><button type="button" onClick={()=>setSelectedId(null)} className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-[var(--nf-surface-2)]" aria-label="إغلاق"><X size={17}/></button></header>
        <div className="p-5"><div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-[var(--nf-green-soft)] p-3 text-center tabular-nums">{[['السعرات',round0(m.kcal),'kcal'],['البروتين',round1(m.p),'غ'],['الكارب',round1(m.c),'غ'],['الدهون',round1(m.f),'غ']].map(([label,value,unit])=><div key={String(label)}><div className="text-[9px] text-[var(--nf-muted)]">{label}</div><b className="mt-1 block text-sm text-[var(--nf-ink)]">{value}<span className="mr-0.5 text-[9px] font-normal">{unit}</span></b></div>)}</div>
          <div className="mt-5 flex items-center justify-between"><span className="text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--nf-ink)]">الكمية</span><span className="text-xs text-[var(--nf-muted)]">خطوة {cfg.step} غ</span></div>
          <div className="mt-2 flex items-center justify-between rounded-2xl border border-[var(--nf-border)] p-2"><button type="button" onClick={()=>setGrams(g=>Math.max(cfg.min,g-cfg.step))} className="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-[var(--nf-surface-2)]"><Minus size={17}/></button><div className="text-center"><div className="text-3xl font-semibold tabular-nums text-[var(--nf-ink)]">{grams}</div><div className="text-[10px] text-[var(--nf-muted)]">غرام</div></div><button type="button" onClick={()=>setGrams(g=>Math.min(cfg.max,g+cfg.step))} className="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-[var(--nf-surface-2)]"><Plus size={17}/></button></div>
          <input type="range" min={cfg.min} max={cfg.max} step={cfg.step} value={clamp(grams,cfg.min,cfg.max)} onChange={e=>setGrams(Number(e.target.value))} className="mt-4 w-full accent-[var(--nf-green)]"/>
          <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-between text-xs"><span className="text-[var(--nf-muted)]">تكلفة هذه الكمية</span><b className="text-base tabular-nums text-[var(--nf-green)]">{round1(m.price)} دج</b></div>
          <div className="mt-4"><div className="mb-2 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--nf-ink)]">أضف إلى</div><div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2"><button type="button" onClick={()=>setPlateUid('new')} className={`min-h-11 rounded-xl border text-xs font-semibold ${plateUid==='new'?'border-[var(--nf-green)] bg-[var(--nf-green-soft)] text-[var(--nf-green)]':'border-[var(--nf-border)]'}`}>طبق جديد</button>{activeItems.filter(item=>item.kind==='custom').map(item=><button key={item.uid} type="button" onClick={()=>setPlateUid(item.uid)} className={`min-h-11 rounded-xl border px-2 text-xs font-semibold ${plateUid===item.uid?'border-[var(--nf-green)] bg-[var(--nf-green-soft)] text-[var(--nf-green)]':'border-[var(--nf-border)]'}`}>الطبق الحالي</button>)}</div></div>
        </div>
        <footer className="border-t border-[var(--nf-border)] p-4 pb-[max(1rem,env(safe-area-inset-bottom))]"><button type="button" onClick={addIngredient} className="flex min-h-13 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-[var(--nf-green)] px-4 text-sm font-bold text-white"><Check size={17}/> أضف المكوّن · {round1(m.price)} دج</button></footer>
      </section>
    </div>;})()}
  </div>;
};


==========================================================================================
FILE: src/components/SvgRing.tsx
==========================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { clamp } from '../utils/nutrition';

interface SvgRingProps {
  progress: number; // 0..1
  size: number;
  strokeWidth?: number;
  color: string;
  trackColor?: string;
  children?: React.ReactNode;
}

export const SvgRing: React.FC<SvgRingProps> = ({
  progress,
  size,
  strokeWidth = 9,
  color,
  trackColor = '#232329',
  children,
}) => {
  const radius = size / 2 - strokeWidth;
  const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * radius;
  const clamped = clamp(progress, 0, 1);
  const offset = circumference * (1 - clamped);

  return (
    <div
      className="relative inline-flex items-center justify-center shrink-0"
      style={{ width: size, height: size }}
    >
      <svg width={size} height={size} className="-rotate-90 block">
        <circle
          cx={size / 2}
          cy={size / 2}
          r={radius}
          fill="none"
          stroke={trackColor}
          strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
        />
        <circle
          cx={size / 2}
          cy={size / 2}
          r={radius}
          fill="none"
          stroke={color}
          strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
          strokeLinecap="round"
          strokeDasharray={circumference}
          strokeDashoffset={offset}
          style={{ transition: 'stroke-dashoffset 0.7s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1)' }}
        />
      </svg>
      <div className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center">
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { AppState, AppTab } from '../types';
import { algiersNow, calcCartTotals, calcGaps, calcNutrition, formatArabicDate, getNextDelivery, round0 } from '../utils/nutrition';
import { MealSlotsSummary } from './MealSlotsSummary';
import { ArrowLeft, Camera, CheckCircle2, Clock3, Flame, Plus, Scale, ShieldCheck, ShoppingBag, UtensilsCrossed } from 'lucide-react';

interface TodayViewProps {
  state: AppState;
  onNavigate: (tab: AppTab) => void;
  onSelectMealSlot: (slotIdx: number) => void;
  onReopenCalc: () => void;
}

const Metric: React.FC<{label:string; value:number; target:number; unit:string; tone:string}> = ({label,value,target,unit,tone}) => {
  const pct = target > 0 ? Math.min(100, Math.max(0, value / target * 100)) : 0;
  return <div className="min-w-0 rounded-2xl border border-[var(--nf-border)] bg-[var(--nf-surface)] p-4">
    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2"><span className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--nf-muted)]">{label}</span><span className="text-[10px] text-[var(--nf-dim)]">الهدف {round0(target)}{unit}</span></div>
    <div className="mt-3 flex items-baseline gap-1"><strong className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-[var(--nf-ink)] tabular-nums">{round0(value)}</strong><span className="text-xs text-[var(--nf-muted)]">{unit}</span></div>
    <div className="mt-3 h-1.5 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-[var(--nf-surface-2)]"><div className="h-full rounded-full transition-[width] duration-300" style={{width:`${pct}%`,background:tone}} /></div>
    <div className="mt-2 text-[10px] text-[var(--nf-muted)] tabular-nums">{round0(Math.max(0,target-value))}{unit} متبقية</div>
  </div>;
};

export const TodayView: React.FC<TodayViewProps> = ({ state, onNavigate, onSelectMealSlot, onReopenCalc }) => {
  const [, setTick] = useState(0);
  useEffect(() => { const id = setInterval(() => setTick(t => t + 1), 30000); return () => clearInterval(id); }, []);
  const res = calcNutrition(state);
  const now = algiersNow();
  const cutoffHour = state.pricingConfig?.cutoffHour ?? 16;
  const dlv = getNextDelivery(cutoffHour);
  const totals = calcCartTotals(state);
  const gaps = calcGaps(state);

  if (res.err) return <section className="rounded-3xl border border-[var(--nf-border)] bg-[var(--nf-surface)] p-6 sm:p-8">
    <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--nf-muted)]">{formatArabicDate(now.d)}</div>
    <h1 className="mt-3 text-2xl font-semibold text-[var(--nf-ink)]">لنبدأ من بيانات صحيحة</h1>
    <p className="mt-2 max-w-xl text-sm leading-7 text-[var(--nf-muted)]">{res.err}</p>
    <button type="button" onClick={onReopenCalc} className="touch-press mt-6 inline-flex min-h-12 items-center gap-3 rounded-xl bg-[var(--nf-green)] px-5 text-sm font-bold text-white">مراجعة الملف الغذائي <ArrowLeft size={16}/></button>
  </section>;

  const usedCap = state.ord.filter(o => o.date === dlv.date && o.status === 'C').reduce((acc,o) => acc + o.n,0);
  const remainingCap = Math.max(0,state.cap-usedCap);
  const latestOrder = state.ord.length ? state.ord[state.ord.length-1] : null;
  const hoursLeft = Math.floor(dlv.minutesLeft/60);
  const minsLeft = dlv.minutesLeft%60;
  const cutoffLabel = `${String(dlv.cutoffHour).padStart(2,'0')}:00`;
  const completion = res.kcal > 0 ? Math.min(100, totals.kcal/res.kcal*100) : 0;

  return <div className="space-y-6 sm:space-y-8">
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
      <div><div className="text-xs font-medium text-[var(--nf-muted)]">{formatArabicDate(now.d)} <span className="mx-1 text-[var(--nf-dim)]">·</span> توقيت الجزائر</div>
        <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-[var(--nf-ink)] sm:text-4xl">مساحتك الغذائية<span className="text-[var(--nf-sage)]">.</span></h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-[var(--nf-muted)]">أهلًا {state.name || 'بك'}، خطتك تبدأ من هنا.</p></div>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-[var(--nf-border)] bg-[var(--nf-surface)] px-3 py-2 text-[11px] font-semibold text-[var(--nf-muted)]"><ShieldCheck size={14} className="text-[var(--nf-green)]"/> تخطيط مبني على بياناتك</div>
    </div>

    <div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-[1.45fr_.85fr]">
      <section className="relative isolate overflow-hidden rounded-[26px] bg-[var(--nf-green)] p-5 text-white sm:p-7">
        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute -left-16 -top-24 -z-10 h-64 w-64 rounded-full border border-white/10"/>
        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute -left-4 -top-12 -z-10 h-44 w-44 rounded-full border border-white/10"/>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-4">
          <div><div className="text-[10px] font-bold tracking-[.2em] text-white/60">YOUR DAILY NUTRITION</div><h2 className="mt-3 text-xl font-semibold sm:text-2xl">تقدّمك اليومي</h2><p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-6 text-white/65">تتبّع الخطة قبل اعتماد وجبات الغد.</p></div>
          <div className="rounded-full border border-white/20 bg-white/10 px-3 py-1.5 text-[10px] font-semibold text-white/80">الهدف اليومي</div>
        </div>
        <div className="mt-8 grid items-end gap-5 sm:grid-cols-[1fr_auto]">
          <div><div className="flex items-end gap-2"><strong className="text-5xl font-semibold tracking-tight tabular-nums">{round0(gaps.kcal)}</strong><span className="pb-1 text-sm text-white/70">kcal متبقية</span></div><div className="mt-4 h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-white/15"><div className="h-full rounded-full bg-[#d9c5a2] transition-[width] duration-500" style={{width:`${completion}%`}}/></div><div className="mt-2 flex justify-between text-[10px] text-white/65"><span>المخطّط {round0(totals.kcal)} kcal</span><span>الهدف {round0(res.kcal)} kcal</span></div></div>
          <div className="min-w-28 border-r border-white/15 pr-5 text-left"><div className="text-[10px] text-white/60">نسبة التخطيط</div><div className="mt-1 text-3xl font-semibold tabular-nums">{round0(completion)}<span className="text-base">%</span></div></div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="flex flex-col justify-between rounded-[26px] border border-[var(--nf-border)] bg-[var(--nf-surface)] p-5 sm:p-6">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between"><span className="text-xs font-bold text-[var(--nf-muted)]">موعد الطلب القادم</span><Clock3 size={17} className="text-[var(--nf-brass)]"/></div>
        <div className="mt-5"><div className="text-xl font-semibold text-[var(--nf-ink)]">{formatArabicDate(dlv.date)}</div><div className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--nf-muted)]">التوصيل القادم المتوقع</div></div>
        <div className="mt-5 flex items-end justify-between gap-3 border-t border-[var(--nf-border)] pt-4"><div><div className="text-[10px] text-[var(--nf-muted)]">إغلاق استقبال الطلبات · {cutoffLabel}</div><div className="mt-1 text-lg font-semibold tabular-nums text-[var(--nf-ink)]">{dlv.closedToday ? 'انتهت النافذة' : `${hoursLeft}س ${minsLeft}د`}</div></div><div className="text-left"><div className="text-[10px] text-[var(--nf-muted)]">سعة المعاينة</div><div className="mt-1 text-sm font-semibold tabular-nums text-[var(--nf-green)]">{remainingCap} / {state.cap}</div></div></div>
      </section>
    </div>

    <section><div className="mb-3 flex items-end justify-between gap-3"><div><h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-[var(--nf-ink)]">مؤشرات التغذية</h2><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--nf-muted)]">القيم المحسوبة مقابل ما خططت له في وجباتك.</p></div><button type="button" onClick={() => onNavigate('me')} className="text-xs font-bold text-[var(--nf-green)]">تفاصيل الحساب <ArrowLeft size={13} className="mr-1 inline"/></button></div>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3"><Metric label="البروتين" value={totals.p} target={res.fp} unit="غ" tone="#24483d"/><Metric label="الكربوهيدرات" value={totals.c} target={res.C} unit="غ" tone="#87978a"/><Metric label="الدهون" value={totals.f} target={res.F} unit="غ" tone="#b28a56"/></div>
    </section>

    <div className="grid items-start gap-4 lg:grid-cols-[1.05fr_.95fr]">

        <div className="mt-5"><MealSlotsSummary state={state} onSelectSlot={onSelectMealSlot}/></div>
        <button type="button" onClick={() => onSelectMealSlot(0)} className="touch-press mt-5 flex min-h-12 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-dashed border-[var(--nf-sage)] bg-[var(--nf-green-soft)] text-xs font-bold text-[var(--nf-green)]"><Plus size={15}/> أضف إلى خطة الوجبات</button>

          {state.meals.length === 0 ? <><h2 className="mt-4 text-lg font-semibold text-[var(--nf-ink)]">ابدأ ببناء وجبتك الأولى</h2><p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-6 text-[var(--nf-muted)]">اختر مكوناتك وحدد أوزانها، وسترى أثر كل تعديل على القيم الغذائية مباشرة.</p><button type="button" onClick={() => onSelectMealSlot(0)} className="touch-press mt-5 flex min-h-12 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-[var(--nf-green)] text-xs font-bold text-white">ابدأ تصميم الوجبات <ArrowLeft size={15}/></button></> : gaps.P > 5 || gaps.C > 10 || gaps.F > 5 || gaps.kcal > 80 ? <><h2 className="mt-4 text-lg font-semibold text-[var(--nf-ink)]">أكمل توزيع احتياجك</h2><p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-6 text-[var(--nf-muted)]">المتبقي: {round0(gaps.P)}غ بروتين، {round0(gaps.C)}غ كربوهيدرات، {round0(gaps.F)}غ دهون.</p><button type="button" onClick={() => onNavigate('menu')} className="mt-4 text-xs font-bold text-[var(--nf-green)]">استكشف المكونات <ArrowLeft size={13} className="mr-1 inline"/></button></> : <div className="mt-4 flex items-start gap-3"><CheckCircle2 className="mt-1 text-[var(--nf-ok)]" size={20}/><div><h2 className="font-semibold text-[var(--nf-ink)]">الخطة قريبة من هدفك</h2><p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-6 text-[var(--nf-muted)]">راجع تفاصيل الوجبات قبل تأكيد الطلب.</p></div></div>}

      </div>

  </div>;

          <p className="mt-3 max-w-sm text-[12px] leading-7 text-[#96938a]">نربط بين بياناتك، احتياجك، اختيارك، والتحضير الفعلي في مسار واحد واضح.</p>
        </div>
        <div id="experience" className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
          {principles.map(({ icon: Icon, title, detail }, index) => (
            <article key={title} className="rounded-2xl border border-[#232329] bg-[#111114] p-5 transition hover:border-[#c8a96e]/35">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-[#18181c] border border-[#232329] text-[#e6d1a3]"><Icon size={17} strokeWidth={1.6} /></span>
                <span className="text-[10px] tracking-[.16em] text-[#66645e] tabular-nums">0{index + 1}</span>
              </div>
              <h3 className="mt-5 text-[14px] font-semibold text-[#f3f1ec]">{title}</h3>
              <p className="mt-2 text-[11px] leading-6 text-[#96938a]">{detail}</p>
            </article>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>

    <footer className="relative z-10 mx-auto flex max-w-6xl flex-col gap-3 px-5 py-7 text-[11px] text-[#66645e] sm:px-8 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">

      <span>الدقة في التخطيط. العناية في التحضير.</span>
    </footer>
  </main>
);

export function createInitialState(): AppState {
  const now = new Date();
  const fmt = (daysAgo: number) => {
    const d = new Date(now.getTime() - daysAgo * 86400000);
    return d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  };
  return {
    ver: STATE_VERSION,
    ob: 0,
    name: '',
    sex: 'm',
    age: 28,
    h: 178,
    w: 82.4,
    targetWeight: 80,
    lbm: 0,
    rel: 0,
    lbmMethod: 'none',
    band: 'A',
    pal: 1.7,
    goal: 'recomp',
    pct: -10,
    al: [],
    fl: { preg: false, kid: false, dia: false, ed: false },
    sup: false,
    supg: 30,
    foods: seedFoods(),
    dishes: seedDishes(),
    zones: DEFAULT_ZONES.map((z) => ({ ...z })),
    pricingConfig: { ...DEFAULT_PRICING },
    meals: [],
    n: 3,
    activeSlot: 0,
    mealNames: [...DEFAULT_MEAL_NAMES],
    zoneId: 'z1',
    orderNote: '',
    ord: [],
    jr: [],
    photos: [],
    cap: 60,
    adh: 92,
    cal: [],
    calDismissedDate: null,
    tab: 'home',
    adminMode: false,
    menuFilter: 'dishes',
    ingFilter: 'all',
    menuSearch: '',
    chartRange: '1m',
  };
}

export const API_ENABLED = import.meta.env.VITE_API_ENABLED === 'true';
export const API_BASE = (import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL || '').replace(/\/+$/,'');

export interface ApiUser {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  email: string;
  role: 'customer' | 'admin' | 'kitchen' | 'delivery';
}
export interface ApiSession {
  token: string;
  user: ApiUser;
}
export async function apiRequest<T>(
  path: string,
  token?: string,
  init: RequestInit = {}
): Promise<T> {
  const headers = new Headers(init.headers);
  if (init.body && !headers.has('Content-Type')) headers.set('Content-Type','application/json');
  if (token) headers.set('Authorization','Bearer ' + token);
  const response = await fetch(API_BASE + path,{...init,headers});
  const payload = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(typeof payload?.error === 'string' ? payload.error : 'تعذر الاتصال بالخدمة.');
  }
  return payload as T;
}

export async function compressProgressImage(file: File): Promise<string> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onerror = () => reject(new Error('تعذر قراءة الصورة'));
    reader.onload = () => {
      const img = new Image();
      img.onerror = () => reject(new Error('تنسيق الصورة غير صالح'));
      img.onload = () => {
        const maxDim = 900;
        let { width, height } = img;
        if (width > maxDim || height > maxDim) {
          if (width >= height) {
            height = Math.round((height * maxDim) / width);
            width = maxDim;
          } else {
            width = Math.round((width * maxDim) / height);
            height = maxDim;
          }
        }
        const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        canvas.width = width;
        canvas.height = height;
        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
        if (!ctx) {
          reject(new Error('تعذر تهيئة معالج الصور'));
          return;
        }
        ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, width, height);
        resolve(canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.72));
      };
      img.src = String(reader.result);
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  });
}


==========================================================================================
FILE: src/utils/media.ts

import proteinImg from '../assets/images/dish_atelier_protein_1794529578370.jpg';
import carbsImg from '../assets/images/dish_atelier_carbs_1794529578367.jpg';
import fatsImg from '../assets/images/dish_atelier_fats_1794529579357.jpg';
import greensImg from '../assets/images/dish_atelier_greens_1794529579750.jpg';
import juicesImg from '../assets/images/dish_atelier_juices_1794529580140.jpg';
import { DishItem, FoodCategory, FoodItem } from '../types';

export const CATEGORY_IMAGES: Record<FoodCategory, string> = {
  protein: proteinImg,
  carb: carbsImg,
  fat: fatsImg,
  veg: greensImg,
  juice: juicesImg,
};

export const CATEGORY_LABELS: Record<FoodCategory, string> = {
  protein: 'بروتين صافي',
  carb: 'مصدر طاقة وكارب',
  fat: 'دهون صحية',
  veg: 'خضر وألياف',
  juice: 'عصير طبيعي',
};

export function getFoodImage(food?: FoodItem | null): string {
  if (!food) return proteinImg;
  if (food.imageUrl && food.imageUrl.trim()) return food.imageUrl.trim();
  return CATEGORY_IMAGES[food.cat] || proteinImg;
}

export function getDishImage(dish: DishItem | null | undefined, foods: FoodItem[]): string {
  if (!dish) return proteinImg;
  if (dish.imageUrl && dish.imageUrl.trim()) return dish.imageUrl.trim();
  // Use the primary protein or first ingredient category photo
  for (const [fid] of dish.ing) {
    const f = foods.find((x) => x.id === fid);
    if (f?.cat === 'protein') return getFoodImage(f);
  }
  const first = foods.find((x) => x.id === dish.ing[0]?.[0]);
  return first ? getFoodImage(first) : proteinImg;
}
